- **check-standards**: 关键规范自动核对（5.1 编码完成后的强制独立节点，始终执行）。触发词：规范核对/自动核对/check-standards/兜底核对/规范检查/产物命名矫正/文件改名归位。用法：/check-standards &lt;项目路径&gt;。作用：**先与用户确认处理范围（新代码 / 全项目）、判模式（prompt 说"按本框架规范"→ 强制标准模式）、（项目配了 Spotless 则先 `mvn spotless:apply` 自动修格式）**，再矫正中间产物命名与路径（技术方案 3.x.1 / 接口清单 3.x.2 / 核对报告 5.2.x / 需求覆盖报告 5.3.x，去任务 ID 前缀，移入模块版本目录），再加载 check-standards skill **优先跑 `scripts/check_standards.py` 机械扫描（无 Python 回退 grep/ast-grep）**生成代码核对关键规范，输出证据报告（5.2.&lt;功能项序号&gt;-规范核对报告.md）；未到位项提示用户确认是否补齐。**轻量模式 / quick-change 增量轻量走收窄清单（见 ai-dev-workflow 方式三），脚本照跑、模型只人工判几项，仍执行不豁免**。**5.3 /accept 已改为用户可选，本节点不再作为"拒绝 5.3"的硬前置（旧 5.3→5.2 自锁已废除）。** ([soft6096/jee-forge](https://github.com/soft6096/jee-forge/blob/HEAD/skill/ai-dev-workflow/commands/check-standards.md) · ★5)

- **checkout**: `grit checkout` makes a working repository from a server's newest commit without downloading its history. It asks the server for a **snapshot**: the target branch's tree (the branch the server's `HEAD` names), cut down to the paths you give, in a single commit. It downloads that snapshot and builds a normal Git repository around it: `git` and `grit` work in it as usual. - **ci**: The pull request's review has passed. The implementer checked only what it changed, so the project's full validation is still to come, and the project's CI setting says where it comes from. - **ci**: The pull request's review has passed. CI is the project's full validation: the implementer checked only what it changed. The merge queue judges CI at the head before it merges: it waits on pending checks, sends red the change caused back to the build queue, and reruns red elsewhere once. So you never wait for CI.
